  ./docs/telos-engine/08_SATTVA_ECONOMICS.md:31:**B Corp Impact Assessment (BIA)**: The [2025 B Corp standards revision](https://usca.bcorporation.net/environmental-stewardship-and-circularity-standards-simplified/) requires GHG disclosure, climate action plans, and science-based targets.   ./dharma_swarm/context.py:1331:        Complete context string for injection into system prompt.
  ./docs/_archive/2026-04/agent_runner_audit.md:14:`agent_runner.py` is the execution core of the dharma_swarm agent system. It contains two primary classes — `AgentRunner` (manages the lifecycle and LLM execution of a single agent) and `AgentPool` (manages a fleet of runners). The module receives tasks from the orchestrator, builds system prompts (injecting dharmic context, memory, stigmergy, and fitness feedback), invokes LLM providers through a `ModelRouter` or direct `CompletionProvider`, handles a multi-round local tool loop (read/write/edit/shell/grep/glob), performs semantic quality assessment with repair retries, and then records results across ~10 subsystems: stigmergy marks, signal bus fitness events, economic spine token tracking, telic seam ontology, agent registry, lineage graph, conversation memory, mem-action extraction, observability traces (Langfuse/jikoku), and guardrail checks. It is the sole bridge between the orchestrator's task queue and actual LLM inference. If this module fails, no agent can execute any task, and every downstream feedback loop (evolution, organism, consolidation, witness, training flywheel) is broken.

  ./docs/archive/specs_research_living_layers/research_shakti_creative_autonomy.md:259:**Connection to Shakti Layer:** This paper reveals both the promise and the present limits of LLM-native open-endedness. The Shakti Layer can be implemented as a system prompt addition (the paper uses "programmed curiosity" via system prompt), but genuine novelty in goal generation requires either fine-tuning or architectural changes.   ./docs/archive/specs_research_living_layers/research_subconscious_ai.md:222:**Connection to HUM Layer:** SRC provides the *biological analogy* for the HUM Layer with the most precise mechanistic detail. The sleep phase is *not deliberate reasoning* — it is autonomous, offline, unsupervised spontaneous activity that produces useful reorganization as a side effect. The HUM Layer recapitulates this: it runs autonomously without task direction, sampling randomly across the SWARM's knowledge corpus, and the "useful reorganization" is the discovery of cross-artifact associations that appear in its output stream.

  docs/archive/specs_research_living_layers/research_stigmergy_agents.md:484:Heylighen explicitly compares stigmergy to neural network coordination: **"Stigmergy: you just leave a message in a medium that everybody can read. Neural networks: you send a particular message to one or more particular agents, and if that's the right agents, the connection is reinforced."** He argues the internet is stigmergic at the ideational level (ideas posted publicly, anyone can respond) while becoming more neural at the infrastructure level (targeted connections).
  docs/archive/specs_research_living_layers/research_stigmergy_agents.md:542:**Living Lattice Connection:** MIDST shows that stigmergy works for knowledge work (not just physical construction or path optimization). Data science work — reading data, transforming it, drawing conclusions — is isomorphic to DHARMA's agent knowledge processing.

  docs/archive/specs_research_living_layers/research_subconscious_ai.md:351:### 4.3 Global Workspace Theory — The Broadcast Model of Consciousness
  docs/archive/specs_research_living_layers/research_subconscious_ai.md:353:**What it is:** Global Workspace Theory (GWT), developed by Bernard Baars and extended by Stanislas Dehaene, proposes that consciousness arises from a brain-wide "workspace" where information becomes globally available to specialized unconscious modules. Unconscious processing happens in parallel, specialized modules. Consciousness is what happens when a winner from the competition among modules gets *broadcast* to the entire system.

  docs/missions/anthropic-economic-futures-submission-2026-03-21/anthropic_grant_application_submission_ready_2026-03-21.md:169:**The zero-kills-all enforcement mechanism**: Because the formula is multiplicative, a project that sequesters carbon but employs no workers ($E = 0$) scores zero welfare-tons. A project that sequesters carbon but displaces the community ($A = 0$) scores zero. A project with unverified claims ($V = 0$) scores zero. This design prevents greenwashing by any single high-performing dimension from masking deficits in others.

  docs/telos-engine/04_MEMETIC_ENGINEERING.md:77:**Emotional contagion is real and measurable**: The Facebook emotional contagion study (Kramer, Guillory, & Hancock, 2014 — controversial for its ethics, not its findings) showed that emotional states propagate through social networks. When Facebook's News Feed algorithm reduced positive posts in users' feeds, those users subsequently posted fewer positive and more negative posts, and vice versa. Emotions are memetically contagious. **Implication**: The emotional valence of Telos Engine content matters as much as its intellectual content. Truth wrapped in despair spreads poorly. Truth wrapped in grounded hope spreads well.
  docs/telos-engine/04_MEMETIC_ENGINEERING.md:164:**Why Palantir matters for the Telos Engine**: Palantir demonstrates that memetic power can be exercised without mass public narrative. Institutional adoption — getting your framework embedded in the decision-making infrastructure of powerful organizations — is a memetic strategy that operates at a different level than consumer adoption. The Telos Engine could pursue a similar path: embedding dharmic decision-making frameworks in institutional contexts.
  docs/telos-engine/04_MEMETIC_ENGINEERING.md:233:**Philosophical frameworks**:
  docs/telos-engine/04_MEMETIC_ENGINEERING.md:258:- **Scientific method**: A memeplex that includes empiricism, peer review, falsifiability, and replication. It is self-correcting — built-in mechanisms (replication, peer review) detect and eliminate false memes within the complex. It spreads because it works (success bias) and because it confers prestige on its practitioners (prestige bias).
  docs/telos-engine/04_MEMETIC_ENGINEERING.md:368:3. **It scales**: Anekantavada works at every level — interpersonal disagreements, political polarization, civilizational clash. It is a universal solvent for memetic rigidity.
  docs/telos-engine/04_MEMETIC_ENGINEERING.md:370:4. **It is self-consistent**: Unlike most meta-cognitive frameworks, anekantavada does not exempt itself from its own analysis. "Anekantavada is the complete truth" would violate anekantavada. It contains its own limitation principle.

  docs/telos-engine/04_MEMETIC_ENGINEERING.md:448:**Educational content (courses, tutorials, workshops)**:
  docs/telos-engine/04_MEMETIC_ENGINEERING.md:534:Small actors can occupy structural positions in information networks that large actors cannot. A lone researcher can bridge between the academic mech-interp community, the contemplative science community, the AI safety community, and the open-source community. A large corporation is siloed into its institutional identity. **Bridging positions in networks have disproportionate influence on information flow** (Burt's structural holes theory).
  docs/telos-engine/04_MEMETIC_ENGINEERING.md:550:**Compound interest of credibility**: Every accurate prediction, every verified claim, every working system adds to a credibility account that grows exponentially. Early credibility growth is slow (almost no one is watching), but the compound curve eventually dominates. Paul Graham's "do things that don't scale" is advice about building compound credibility before you have the resources for broad distribution.

## AI-D1 - Unproven dependency provenance
